# Release v0.26.0

- Title: v0.26.0
- URL: https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/releases/tag/v0.26.0
- Tag commit: `d7cb574130e6f01ad25b3289685489200febcd74`
- Published: 2026-10-05 16:36 CEST
- Prerelease: false

## Overview

ggml v0.26.0 brings a new `alloc_buffer_n`/`get_alloc_size_n` buffer allocation API, sparse flash attention kernels on SYCL, Vulkan and Metal, and major lightning indexer improvements (halved score memory, tiling, MUSA support). The CPU backend gains BF16 ops and a tiled k-quant mul_mat, CUDA gains a model-driven W4A4 (NVFP4/MXFP4) mul_mat path plus MMVQ shared-expert fusion, and the Hexagon backend adds a sampler and more quant types. Model loading is faster with stricter GGUF size validation, Windows ARM64 MSVC builds are enabled, and WebGPU/OpenVINO/OpenCL/SYCL pick up numerous new kernels, ops and fixes.

### API changes

- Add `alloc_buffer_n` and `get_alloc_size_n` to the buffer type interface with public APIs `ggml_backend_buft_alloc_buffer_n`/`ggml_backend_buft_get_alloc_size_n` for multi-buffer allocation and size planning ([llama/23671](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/23671))
- Add new public header `include/ggml-zdnn.h` for the zDNN backend ([llama/29541](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29541))

### Core changes

- Speed up model loading and fix a hang on crafted GGUFs with very large KV dimensions ([llama/29598](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29598))
- Harden tensor/GGUF size validation: fix integer overflows and reject tensor sizes that wrap after padding ([llama/29384](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29384), [llama/26979](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26979))
- Collect all input tensors into `graph_inputs` up front and require them to be `GGML_OP_NONE`, fixing spurious graph re-reserves with pipeline parallelism ([llama/29634](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29634), [llama/29647](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29647))
- Meta backend: clear inactive AllReduce shards with FILL instead of SCALE ([llama/29793](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29793))
- ggml-quants: avoid invalid rounding in the qkx3 scale search ([llama/29817](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29817))
- Enable Windows ARM64 builds with MSVC cl.exe ([llama/28362](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28362))

### Backend changes

#### CPU

- Support BF16/FP16/FP32 K tails in tinyBLAS on x86 ([llama/29806](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29806))
- BF16 op support: add unary, GLU, binary and scale ops (with CUDA) and accept BF16 in src1 of mul_mat ([llama/29675](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29675), [llama/28937](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28937))
- Tiled mul_mat for k-quants via int8 unpack tiles and 16x16 microkernels ([llama/27851](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27851))
- Enable tiled flash attention for non-vector-multiple head dims on x86 ([llama/29423](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29423))
- Accumulate f16 dot products in f32 on AVX512-FP16 ([llama/29545](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29545))
- Add Q8_0 IME1 matrix kernel for SpacemiT X60 ([llama/28479](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28479))
- Fix soft_max_back wrong output when dst aliases src1 ([llama/27096](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27096))

#### CUDA / HIP

- Add model-driven W4A4 (NVFP4/MXFP4) mul_mat path with `llama_prec_policy` ([llama/24364](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/24364))
- NVFP4: optimize MMQ accumulation and handle the compute type on the cuBLAS path ([llama/29857](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29857), [llama/29173](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29173))
- Fuse shared experts into MMVQ ([llama/29184](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29184))
- Use MMVF for thin f16/bf16 mul_mat at small batch size ([llama/29633](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29633))
- FlashAttention: prefer whole-tile scheduling for two-stage kernels, tune fp16 tile configs for head sizes 40-112, fix 2 broken Volta cases ([llama/29435](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29435), [llama/26289](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26289), [llama/29803](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29803))
- Lightning indexer: halve the score memory (CUDA/Metal/Vulkan) and tile over keys and tokens for 4 heads ([llama/29825](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29825), [llama/29901](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29901))
- Fuse RMS_NORM + SCALE into one kernel ([llama/29393](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29393))
- Bitonic argsort handles rows wider than one block ([llama/28957](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28957))
- Add F16 support to FWHT and a F16 kernel for CONV_2D_DW ([llama/29096](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29096), [llama/29064](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29064))
- SSM_SCAN: support state size 96 ([llama/28717](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28717))
- Fixes: batch-independent alloc_deps check, unexpected graph reallocation in k-pool models, MMQ memory fault when n_expert >> n_ubatch, cpy transposed path corrupting non-contiguous dst ([llama/29986](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29986), [llama/29958](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29958), [llama/29941](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29941), [llama/27663](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27663))
- Make the CCCL version configurable and pin it for CI ([llama/29792](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29792))
- HIP: enable the fattn-mma kernel on CDNA for dkq > 256 at large batch sizes, fixing template skip and DGX Spark misdetection ([llama/28907](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28907), [llama/29559](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29559), [llama/29572](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29572))
- HIP: optimize packed byte subtraction ([llama/29478](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29478))
- MUSA: fix PH1 (MTT S5000) operator and build issues, use the vector lightning indexer kernel ([llama/29193](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29193), [llama/29990](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29990))

#### Metal

- Flash attention: add tensor API kernel for F16 KV, split FA kernels into per-dtype libraries, optimize sparse FA ([llama/29570](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29570), [llama/29329](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29329), [llama/29377](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29377))
- FWHT: support block widths above 512 with perf optimizations ([llama/29095](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29095), [llama/29602](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29602))
- mul_mat: use bf16 math for mxfp4, add few-row MMA mat-mul ([llama/29770](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29770), [llama/29869](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29869))
- Support left and circular padding in GGML_OP_PAD ([llama/29561](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29561))
- Fix graph capture and handle empty graphs, release temporary private transfer buffers ([llama/29390](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29390), [llama/29777](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29777))

#### Vulkan

- int8 coopmat1 matmul implementation for AMD RDNA3 and RDNA4 ([llama/27952](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27952))
- Sparse flash attention for quantized K/V ([llama/29639](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29639))
- Perf: reuse descriptor sets when bindings are constant, fuse the SCALE→SIGMOID→SCALE→HC_POST chain ([llama/29280](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29280), [llama/29520](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29520))
- Flash attention: fix shmem write out of bounds and stale prealloc_y reuse across FA and soft_max ([llama/29988](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29988), [llama/29591](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29591))
- Fix wrong results when a mul_mat reads a slice of a larger cache ([llama/28956](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28956))
- Tuning: GDN kernel on Intel, RDNA4 mat_vec, disable large matmul tile on Samsung GPUs with 32KB shared memory, argsort kernel selection on Adreno ([llama/29476](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29476), [llama/29934](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29934), [llama/28531](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28531), [llama/29469](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29469))
- Fix build with legacy GLSLC without cooperativeMatrix API ([llama/29409](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29409))

#### WebGPU

- Add MMVQ support for Q1_0/Q5_0/Q5_1/Q3_K/Q5_K/Q6_K/MXFP4 ([llama/29483](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29483))
- Add bfloat16 support for MUL_MAT/MUL_MAT_ID/GET_ROWS and f16 support for fill/set_rows ([llama/29358](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29358), [llama/29897](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29897))
- Fix SSM_SCAN binding aliasing and unaligned writes in buffer_set_tensor ([llama/29750](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29750), [llama/29471](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29471))

#### SYCL

- Add sparse flash attention ([llama/28796](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28796))
- Q8_0 DMMV ESIMD and MMVQ wide load ([llama/29186](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29186))
- FWHT kernels for block widths above 512, large register file for D=512 FA vec kernels ([llama/29243](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29243), [llama/29062](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29062))
- Avoid the slow oneDNN reference matmul and fattn ([llama/28985](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28985))
- Reduce tensor allreduce sync with pinned host buffers ([llama/29604](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29604))

#### OpenCL

- Add Q8_0 non-MoE dp4a and Q5_K gemv binary kernels ([llama/29439](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29439), [llama/29401](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29401))
- Use sigmoid f16 for bf16 ([llama/29787](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29787))
- Adreno: fix get_tensor for Q5_K gemm_nonshuffle kernel, mark vec subgroup bcast as supported for the E17 compiler ([llama/29555](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29555), [llama/29698](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29698))

#### OpenVINO

- Update backend to 2026.4.1: performance optimizations, expanded op support, improved device listing ([llama/29852](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29852))
- Serve GET_ROWS on a weight view from the base Constant ([llama/28381](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28381))

#### Hexagon

- Add backend sampler support and q2_k/q3_k/q5_k quant types, improve the dynamic quantizer ([llama/29502](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29502), [llama/29717](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29717), [llama/29123](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29123), [llama/29395](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29395))
- Add F16 activation ops and FP32 GELU_ERF/GEGLU_ERF ([llama/29209](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29209), [llama/29631](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29631))
- Optimize ALLREDUCE with safe scatter mode, shared strided DMA copy for CPY and CONCAT ([llama/29757](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29757), [llama/29685](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29685))
- Tiled Q4_0/Q8_0 GET_ROWS, I32 CPY/CONT, multi-sequence concat_2d ([llama/29511](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29511), [llama/29379](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29379), [llama/29344](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29344))
- Fix work-queue race condition in seqn vs idx_read/write ([llama/29785](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29785))

#### RPC

- Use the RDMA completion channel to avoid spinning, include nb in the get_alloc_size cache key and floor at ggml_nbytes ([llama/29440](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29440), [llama/29283](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29283))

#### zDNN

- Implement buffer reset, fix memory leaks and a 0-row tensor crash ([llama/29637](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29637), [llama/29636](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29636))

#### BLAS

- Document AOCL-BLAS build and label the device AOCL-BLAS ([llama/29640](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29640))

# Release v0.25.3

- Title: v0.25.3
- URL: https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/releases/tag/v0.25.3
- Tag commit: `353b63b439f27ab2cc19dac97ab1681ba6d2d084`
- Published: 2026-09-24 21:20 CEST
- Prerelease: false

## Overview

A small maintenance release: the core graph size calculation (`ggml_graph_nbytes`) no longer triggers a UBSan "non-zero offset to null pointer" diagnostic, and the CI setup moves CUDA builds to the hf-jobs runner and adds a new AMD Vulkan build job.

### Core changes

- Fix a UBSan error in `ggml_graph_nbytes` by computing the layout relative to a non-null base pointer
  ([ggml#1644](https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/pull/1644))

## More info

- [Releases and versioning of `ggml-org` projects](https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/discussions/1579)

74b191a3 ggml : fix ubsan error in `ggml_graph_nbytes` (#1644)

# Release v0.25.2

- Title: v0.25.2
- URL: https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/releases/tag/v0.25.2
- Tag commit: `a0d15e246b70dcdd5dc2bde32640778d6701732c`
- Published: 2026-09-24 15:05 CEST
- Prerelease: false

## Overview

A small point release focused on backend improvements: a new CUDA convolution kernel with an implicit-GEMM fast path, Vulkan fixes and tuning (misalignment handling in convolution shaders and cooperative-matrix support for Adreno GPUs), an optimized DP4A binary kernel for OpenCL Q6_K GEMM, and a precision guard in the Hexagon backend.

### Backend changes

#### CUDA

- added `conv3d` kernel with an implicit-GEMM fast path (F16 weights) and a direct fallback for F32 shapes (https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29137)

#### Vulkan

- handle misalignment in the `conv_2d` and `conv_3d` matrix-multiply shaders (https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29365)
- enable and tune `VK_KHR_cooperative_matrix` support for Adreno GPUs with hardware matrix cores (https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29328)

#### OpenCL

- added optimized DP4A binary kernel for Q6_K non-MoE GEMM (https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29057)

#### Hexagon

- reject `MUL_MAT_ID` when the `src1` precision is F32 (not supported) (https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29348)


## More info

- [Releases and versioning of `ggml-org` projects](https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/discussions/1579)

# Release v0.25.1

- Title: v0.25.1
- URL: https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/releases/tag/v0.25.1
- Tag commit: `e565a8f4ce2e462c4973a24c51098dd3c81c0256`
- Published: 2026-09-23 19:26 CEST
- Prerelease: false

## Overview

A hotfix release focused on the CUDA backend: sparse flash attention, which had been disabled due to a batch-dependent gate, is re-enabled for long-context prefill (up to ~1.4x prefill speedup at 131k context), with the supporting sparse mask scan kernel significantly sped up. Additionally, Metal gains the missing f32 × bf16 matmul kernels (fixing depthwise convolutions with bf16 weights) and its flash-attention tuning tables are now keyed by GPU family instead of individual SKU, while Vulkan adds MMQ/MMV matmul kernels for the IQ4_XS quantization type.

### Backend changes

#### CUDA

- Re-enable sparse flash attention for long-context prefill (switched off by [llama.cpp#28770](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28770) due to a
  batch-dependent gate), with up to ~1.4x prefill speedup at 131k context ([llama.cpp#29298](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29298))
- Speed up the sparse mask scan kernel via compile-time loop unrolling and host-side bound selection (586 → 244 us
  for the batched sparse op at 49k context) ([llama.cpp#29298](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29298))

#### Metal

- Add the missing f32 × bf16 `mul_mv` kernel variants, fixing depthwise convolutions with bf16 weights ([llama.cpp#28741](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28741))
- Key the flash-attention vector tuned tables by GPU family instead of individual SKU, falling back to the
  baseline for untuned families and substantially shrinking the tuning tables ([llama.cpp#29075](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29075))

#### Vulkan

- Add MMQ/MMV matmul kernels for the IQ4_XS quantization type ([llama.cpp#28415](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28415))

## More info

- [Releases and versioning of `ggml-org` projects](https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/discussions/1579)

## Changelog since v0.25.0

60e21b46 metal: add the missing f32 x bf16 mul_mv variants (llama/28741)
4506ab1d CUDA: enable sparse-fa for dsv4 prefill (again) (llama/29298)
a70078ad metal : key the fa-vec tuned table by family instead of SKU (llama/29075)
8b93b8f0 vulkan: add IQ4_XS MMQ/MMV matmul kernels (llama/28415)

# Release v0.25.0

- Title: v0.25.0
- URL: https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/releases/tag/v0.25.0
- Tag commit: `cae37675560bce40e5e8b7a505cb0cd06d211a5c`
- Published: 2026-09-23 10:33 CEST
- Prerelease: false

## Overview

This release expands hyper-connection, flash-attention, and fused MoE/SSM support across CPU, GPU, and accelerator backends. It also improves backend robustness, quantization, data-layout handling, and RPC/meta buffer management. Numerous correctness fixes and performance tuning land across all major backends, including new kernels, fusions, and op coverage.

### API changes

- Added gated `ggml_dsv4_hc_pre_gated()` ([llama/28901](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28901)).
- Allowed `ggml_dsv4_hc_post()` `NULL` comb ([llama/28901](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28901)).
- Bumped the RPC protocol major version to 7 ([llama/24292](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/24292)).

### Core changes

- Added gated hyper-connection preprocessing ([llama/28901](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28901)).
- Added `hc_post` identity path without comb ([llama/28901](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28901)).
- Fixed dimension/stride truncation in `ggml_permute` ([llama/29227](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29227)).
- Scheduler propagates graph reservation failures ([llama/26070](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26070)).
- Optimized IQ1_M with per-block prefix sums ([llama/28706](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28706)).
- GGUF data alignment is now relative to GGUF start ([llama/28993](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28993)).

### Backend changes

#### CPU

- Added ARM repack kernels for Q1_0 ([llama/23492](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/23492)).
- Fixed SpacemiT int16 transpose ([llama/25161](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/25161)).
- Added F16 input for FWHT and hadamard `MUL_MAT` ([llama/27779](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27779)).

#### CUDA / HIP

- Added implicit-GEMM `conv2d` support ([llama/29135](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29135)).
- Convert contiguous tensors four elements at a time ([llama/29155](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29155)).
- Added i16/i32 `DUP` support ([llama/28897](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28897)).
- Added row-contiguous `SUM_ROWS` support ([llama/26308](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26308)).
- Refactored flash attention shared-memory swizzle ([llama/28536](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28536)).
- Tuned flash attention shapes for Ampere and newer ([llama/29152](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29152)).
- Enabled sparse flash attention ([llama/28770](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28770)).
- Tuned the SM70 MMVQ/MMQ crossover ([llama/28912](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28912)).
- Fixed the sm_70 tile compilation error ([llama/29224](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29224)).
- Fixed CUB argsort corruption from in-place keys ([llama/28389](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28389)).
- Fixed top-k MoE dispatch ([llama/28432](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28432)).
- Improved im2col access patterns ([llama/28013](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28013)).
- HIP: optimized IQ2/IQ3 with SWAR ([llama/27962](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27962)).
- HIP: enabled AllReduce for ROCm ([llama/27825](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27825)).
- HIP: broadened RDNA3.5 MoE tile heuristics ([llama/28935](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28935)).
- HIP: use F32 accumulation for MFMA flash attention ([llama/28576](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28576)).

#### Metal

- Added MoE and SSM_CONV fusion optimizations ([llama/28948](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28948)).
- Added hyper-connection fusion optimizations ([llama/28948](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28948)).
- Added arbitrary `hc` support in `dsv4_hc_pre` ([llama/29169](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29169)).
- Added new hyper-connection ops ([llama/29000](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29000)).
- Added F16 input support to FWHT ([llama/29094](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29094)).
- Added flash attention kernels for new head sizes ([llama/28599](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28599)).
- Fixed flash attention mask bounds ([llama/29220](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29220)).
- Fixed flash attention support checks ([llama/29122](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29122)).
- Fixed `mul_mm_id` NaN for large F16 activations ([llama/26223](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26223)).
- Gated `mul_mm_id` src1 rescale behind `ggml_prec` ([llama/29029](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29029)).
- Fixed macOS SDK deprecation warnings ([llama/29136](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29136)).
- Simplified fusion-pattern declarations ([llama/29206](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29206)).

#### Vulkan

- Added IQ3_S MMQ matmul kernels ([llama/28822](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28822)).
- Added sparse flash attention support ([llama/28105](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28105)).
- Added Intel Xe flash attention optimization kernels ([llama/24406](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/24406)).
- Added new hyper-connection ops ([llama/28988](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28988)).
- Refactored Vulkan buffers/debug into shared modules ([llama/28732](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28732)).
- Raised `mul_mat_id` row-id limit to 512 experts ([llama/28501](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28501)).
- Skipped unneeded MoE work in the coopmat1 path ([llama/25483](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/25483)).
- Fixed buffer reference alignment in im2col shaders ([llama/28996](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28996)).
- Worked around the NV argsort_large compiler bug ([llama/28975](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28975)).
- Made `MUL_MAT_ID` BN/2 tail unconditional ([llama/28923](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28923)).

#### OpenCL

- Added an A8 Q4_0 non-MoE dp4a binary kernel ([llama/29055](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29055)).
- Added an A8 q4_K/q8_1 binary GEMM kernel ([llama/29056](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29056)).
- Added an A8 q6_K binary GEMM kernel ([llama/28678](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28678)).
- Added a binary flash attention kernel ([llama/29046](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29046)).
- Added generic `ssm_scan` support ([llama/28881](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28881)).
- Choose the MoE expert matmul by batch size ([llama/27637](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27637)).

#### SYCL

- Added `GET_ROWS_BACK` support for FP32/FP16 ([llama/25266](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/25266)).
- Added gated `dsv4_hc_pre` support ([llama/29132](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29132)).
- Added optional `hc_post` comb matrix support ([llama/29132](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29132)).
- Added MMVQ GLU fusion ([llama/28931](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28931)).
- Added rms_norm+scale and ssm_conv+silu fusions ([llama/28931](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28931)).
- Fused the SiLU epilogue into the ssm_conv kernel ([llama/28929](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28929)).
- Improved MKL-FA softmax load coalescing ([llama/28918](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28918)).
- Fixed pinned memory to use the right device context ([llama/28895](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28895)).
- Fixed a large-allocation memory error ([llama/28953](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28953)).
- Fixed the split buffer type function signature ([llama/28981](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28981)).
- Added hadamard fp16 unit-test support ([llama/29218](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29218)).

#### Hexagon

- Added an HMX-optimized gated delta net ([llama/29199](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29199)).
- Added Q4_K and Q6_K K-quants support ([llama/28994](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28994)).
- Added `ROLL` op support ([llama/29105](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29105)).
- Added `TOP_K` op support ([llama/29113](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29113)).
- Added `GEGLU_QUICK` op support ([llama/29114](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29114)).
- Added I32 `GET_ROWS` support ([llama/29116](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29116)).
- Overhauled buffer/DMA handling for 64-bit mappings ([llama/29197](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29197)).
- Added direct-mapped DMA cache for FA masks ([llama/29282](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29282)).
- Updated im2col support ([llama/29103](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29103)).
- Added flash attention head-dim padding ([llama/26539](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26539)).
- Restored contiguous fast-paths and `hvx_copy_uu` ([llama/28886](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28886)).
- Use DMA for contiguous hex-cpy ([llama/28906](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28906)).
- Accept the zeroed rope probe in `supports_op` ([llama/28995](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28995)).

#### WebGPU

- Added fused gated-delta-net + copy ([llama/28976](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28976)).
- Fixed `GET_ROWS` support checks ([llama/28978](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28978)).
- Added self-hosted WebGPU CI coverage ([llama/28712](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28712)).

#### OpenVINO

- Updated OpenVINO to 2026.4 ([llama/29009](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29009)).
- Fixed clangd and MSVC warnings ([llama/29009](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29009)).
- Optimized stateful decode and GPU MoE inference ([llama/28638](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28638)).
- Added fused compressed-MoE and KV-state passes ([llama/28638](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28638)).
- Expanded op coverage for rope, norms, MoE, and flash-attention graphs.

#### RPC / Meta

- RPC invalidates cached graphs on buffer free ([llama/24292](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/24292)).
- RPC hashes only weights ([llama/28789](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28789)).
- Meta backend resolves multi-buffer views ([llama/29266](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/29266)).

# Release v0.24.0

- Title: v0.24.0
- URL: https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/releases/tag/v0.24.0
- Tag commit: `456172ec733a135778adcd32d00e576a58232e45`
- Published: 2026-09-14 15:40 CEST
- Prerelease: false

## Overview

This release focuses on expanding backend coverage and robustness, with a new precision-control API, major Vulkan/SYCL/Hexagon/OpenCL work, and numerous correctness and performance fixes across CPU, CUDA, Metal, and other backends.

### API changes

- Expanded `ggml_prec` with `GGML_PREC_BF16`, `F16`, `Q8`, and `Q4`, and deprecated `GGML_PREC_DEFAULT` ([llama/26675](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26675)).
- Added `ggml_prec_set_acc()` and `ggml_prec_set_src()` to control accumulator and per-source precision for `MUL_MAT`/`MUL_MAT_ID` and flash attention ([llama/26675](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26675)).
- Deprecated `ggml_mul_mat_set_prec()` and `ggml_flash_attn_ext_set_prec()` in favor of the new precision API ([llama/26675](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26675)).

### Core changes

- Added precision op-params layout for `MUL_MAT` and `MUL_MAT_ID` in `ggml-impl.h` ([llama/26675](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26675)).
- Backend scheduler no longer forces an extra split for backend inputs and skips zero-sized MoE `ids` tensors ([llama/28387](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28387), [llama/28739](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28739)).
- Added PCH/unity-build support to speed up builds ([llama/28091](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28091)).
- Fixed MSVC+Clang `ggml_vld1q_u32` and added a missing header for `gguf.cpp` ([llama/28284](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28284), [llama/28566](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28566)).

### Backend changes

#### CPU

- Disabled PCH and fixed `CACHE_LINE_SIZE` ambiguity causing heap corruption ([llama/28882](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28882)).
- Added s390x repack support for `q4_0` and Q1_0 vector intrinsics; guarded VXE-only helpers and added non-VXE tests ([llama/28667](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28667), [llama/28606](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28606), [llama/28775](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28775), [llama/28776](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28776)).
- Added PCH support to `ggml-cpu` ([llama/28091](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28091)).
- Fixed MSVC+Clang `ggml_vld1q_u32` ([llama/28284](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28284)).

#### CUDA / HIP / MUSA

- Added AMD GCN-specific MMQ config table and gfx90c HIP support ([llama/27841](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27841), [llama/26454](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26454)).
- Fall back to F32 on devices without BF16 hardware acceleration ([llama/28846](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28846)).
- Reworked flash attention quant compile flags (`GGML_FA_QUANTS`) and tuned flash attention for gfx1201 ([llama/28079](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28079), [llama/28102](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28102)).
- Fixed divergent barrier in f16 flash attention and races in MoE MMID/MMF kernels ([llama/27870](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27870), [llama/28475](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28475)).
- Tuned MoE MMQ N-tile sizing for RDNA3 and added branchless Q4_K/Q5_K unpack with L2 prefetch ([llama/24546](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/24546), [llama/28552](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28552), [llama/26705](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26705)).
- Reverted HIP `prop.integrated` restore ([llama/28604](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28604)).

#### Metal

- Reworked fusion into a single-source table with debug support ([llama/28164](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28164)).
- Added flash attention vec tuning for M2 Max and M3 ([llama/28458](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28458), [llama/28396](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28396)).
- Fixed idle threads in IQ `mul_mv` kernels and skipped empty MoE token tiles ([llama/28692](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28692), [llama/28086](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28086), [llama/28301](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28301)).
- Fixed memory leak in an early-return path ([llama/28399](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28399)).

#### Vulkan

- Added TQ1_0 and iq4_xs support, type-aligned GET_ROWS, and f16 B-type matmul pipelines ([llama/27765](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27765), [llama/28426](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28426), [llama/28253](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28253), [llama/27471](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27471)).
- Added hyper-connection fused ops and RMSNorm/UNARY fusion opportunities ([llama/26578](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26578), [llama/28024](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28024), [llama/27220](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27220)).
- Added command-buffer debug labels and improved allocation dependencies for prefill topk_moe fusion ([llama/28101](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28101), [llama/28422](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28422)).
- Fixed argsort data race/OOB, NV queue-submit driver workaround, FILL workgroup limits, and PowerVR dmmv fallback ([llama/28705](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28705), [llama/28830](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28830), [llama/28592](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28592), [llama/28341](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28341)).
- Use spec constants for MM A-type and added small-M optimizations ([llama/25773](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/25773), [llama/28457](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28457)).
- Fixed 32-bit Vulkan-Hpp handle usage and added CPU write path for async copy when idle ([llama/22892](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/22892), [llama/28618](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28618)).

#### OpenCL

- Added Adreno xmem SDPA path and extended elementwise/data-movement op coverage ([llama/26331](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26331), [llama/27633](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27633)).
- Added A8 `q4_0`/`q4_K` binary GEMM kernels and fixed noshuffle row alignment for q4_K/q5_K/q8_0 ([llama/28268](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28268), [llama/28677](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28677), [llama/28575](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28575)).
- Fixed backend abort bugs, non-contiguous conv2d inputs, and q4_K/q5_K weight pack selection ([llama/27630](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27630), [llama/28503](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28503), [llama/28402](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28402)).

#### SYCL

- Added radix-select top_k, batched L2_NORM, and IQ type handling for MoE ([llama/28670](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28670), [llama/28222](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28222), [llama/28476](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28476)).
- Restored Kronecker product FWHT support and added allocation site tracing (`GGML_SYCL_MEMTRACE`) ([llama/28254](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28254), [llama/27631](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27631)).
- Fixed oneDNN scratchpad pool ordering and memory error handling ([llama/28704](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28704), [llama/28227](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28227)).
- Fail gracefully for unsupported TQ1_0 quants ([llama/28681](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28681)).

#### Hexagon

- Added multi-device model split (row-split) support and rope updates ([llama/28589](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28589), [llama/28628](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28628)).
- Added RELU and LEAKY_RELU ops ([llama/28585](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28585)).

#### WebGPU

- Updated Dawn and aligned tensor bindings to type block size ([llama/28683](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28683), [llama/28382](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28382)).
- Added type-aligned GET_ROWS support ([llama/28253](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28253)).

#### RPC / OpenVINO

- Fixed RPC linking with `BUILD_SHARED_LIBS=OFF` ([llama/28492](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28492)).
- Updated OpenVINO for type-aligned GET_ROWS ([llama/28253](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28253)).

## More info

- [Releases and versioning of `ggml-org` projects](https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/discussions/1579)

# Release v0.23.0

- Title: v0.23.0
- URL: https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/releases/tag/v0.23.0
- Tag commit: `e91ded11bdcd78c42f9c8d3978ff6686eb4c1226`
- Published: 2026-09-04 13:36 CEST
- Prerelease: false

## Overview

Version 0.23.0 adds new operators and backend scheduling APIs for sparse attention, asynchronous execution, and allocation-dependency tracking. It includes broad correctness and performance work across CPU, GPU, and accelerator backends, plus an expanded RPC stack with Apple RDMA support.

### API changes

- Added `GGML_GLU_OP_SWIGLU_CLAMP` and `ggml_swiglu_clamp()` ([llama/27930](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27930)).
- Added `ggml_flash_attn_ext_set_n_kv_max()` for sparse K/V masks ([llama/27970](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27970)).
- RPC protocol bumped to 6.0.0 for event/async APIs ([llama/18626](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/18626)).

### Core changes

- Added SWIGLU_CLAMP and sparse-attention `n_kv_max` support ([llama/27930](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27930), [llama/27970](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27970)).
- Added allocation-size expansion tracking and allocation-dependency support in backend graph optimization ([llama/27960](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27960), [llama/27301](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27301)).
- Fixed multi-batch `conv_transpose_2d` and allowed `SET_ROWS` in backward passes ([llama/26132](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26132)).
- Backend search-path enumeration tolerates filesystem errors; meta buffers propagate usage ([llama/28271](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28271), [llama/27586](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27586)).
- Replaced compile-definition versioning with generated `ggml-version.h` ([llama/28364](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28364)).
- RPC gains event/async APIs, Apple RDMA transport, and avoids serializing remote buffers ([llama/18626](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/18626), [llama/26421](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26421), [llama/26500](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26500)).

### Backend changes

#### CPU

- AVX2 IQ prompt speedups for large batches ([llama/27402](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27402)).
- Fixed s390x Q5_1 uninitialized accumulator and added conditional SpacemiT IME kernels ([llama/28332](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28332), [llama/27961](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27961)).
- Reworked KleidiAI build/integration and avoided buffer-type init on dispatch ([llama/26077](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26077), [llama/27891](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27891)).

#### CUDA / ROCm

- Added sparse FA, XOR-swizzled FA K/V smem, radix top-k, and extended MoE fusions ([llama/27970](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27970), [llama/25635](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/25635), [llama/27466](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27466), [llama/25952](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/25952)).
- Added concurrent streams per split, MMQ/MMVQ tunings, and MoE MMQ fixes ([llama/28198](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28198), [llama/28285](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28285), [llama/26264](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26264)).
- ROCm/hip: tuned RDNA3 MMQ configs and optimized Q2_0 dot products ([llama/26284](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26284), [llama/26753](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26753)).

#### Metal

- Added sparse FA, radix top-k, quantized concat, and chunked SSD MMA ([llama/28098](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28098), [llama/28073](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28073), [llama/28116](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28116), [llama/26647](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26647)).
- Added fa-vec tunings across many Apple GPUs and Metal 4.0 tensor API support ([llama/28373](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28373), [llama/27461](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27461)).
- Added metallib xcframework support and fixed memory, leak, and dispatch issues ([llama/28163](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28163), [llama/27758](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27758), [llama/28306](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28306)).

#### Vulkan

- Added cross-entropy loss, radix top-k, Lightning Indexer op, and IQ3_S batch mat-vec improvements ([llama/27216](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27216), [llama/28032](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28032), [llama/27453](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27453), [llama/27449](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27449)).
- Fixed FA dequant, mul_mat_id padding, view-alias deps, and warp-size handling ([llama/28190](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28190), [llama/27925](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27925), [llama/27812](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27812), [llama/27726](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27726)).
- Added shader hoisting for row IDs/expert counts and bfloat16 extension gating ([llama/26686](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26686), [llama/28155](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28155)).

#### SYCL

- Added residual fusions, peer-to-peer copy, and host-pinned memory limits ([llama/27610](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27610), [llama/27550](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27550), [llama/27559](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27559)).
- Added GPU free-memory query, split TOP_K long rows, TILE quantized KV decode, and oneDNN SDPA f16 KV binding ([llama/27968](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27968), [llama/27847](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27847), [llama/26689](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26689), [llama/27468](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27468)).
- Reduced redundant Q4_K MMVQ work and improved `--fit` handling ([llama/27062](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27062), [llama/27629](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27629)).

#### OpenCL

- Added quantized lm_head/decode GEMV and medium-batch GEMM optimizations ([llama/26477](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26477)).
- Added Adreno fixes and matmul path improvements, Intel Xe-LP quant tuning, and MoE binary kernels ([llama/27640](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27640), [llama/26438](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26438), [llama/27768](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27768)).

#### Hexagon

- Added F16 unary ops, ABS/LOG, MUL_MAT/MUL_MAT_ID fusion, and CPY fence fixes ([llama/28228](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28228), [llama/27786](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27786), [llama/28202](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28202), [llama/28033](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28033)).
- Added multi-NPU device support, fully async backend, and on-demand device discovery ([llama/26501](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26501), [llama/27785](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27785)).

#### OpenVINO

- Updated OpenVINO, added new op support, whisper.cpp support, and a fuse-to-conv pass ([llama/27843](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27843)).

#### RPC

- Implemented event and async backend APIs and Apple RDMA transport ([llama/18626](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/18626), [llama/26421](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26421)).
- Avoided serializing remote buffers and fixed pre-RDMA macOS teardown/version issues ([llama/26500](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26500), [llama/27815](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27815), [llama/27908](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27908)).

#### WebGPU

- Fixed misaligned offset crash in tensor get and marked MUL_MAT for allocation expansion ([llama/28045](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28045), [llama/28071](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28071)).

## More info

- [Releases and versioning of `ggml-org` projects](https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/discussions/1579)

# Release v0.22.0

- Title: v0.22.0
- URL: https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/releases/tag/v0.22.0
- Tag commit: `34dc0e5589504286cb40e13cbdae4bf2b5b4071b`
- Published: 2026-08-25 10:44 CEST
- Prerelease: false

   ## Overview                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           
   This release adds tensor-split support to the multi-backend (meta) backend with improved split-state propagation, reworks the Metal kernels into per-op sources with parallel compilation, and fixes `ggml_clamp` to be a proper non-in-place op. It also brings new ops (`POOL_1D`, `PAD_REFLECT_1D`), Q2_K SYCL kernels, MoE bias fusion on OpenCL, and assorted fixes across the CUDA, Metal, SYCL, Vulkan, OpenCL and WebGPU backends.                                                                                                                                     
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           
   ### API changes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           
   - `ggml_clamp` is now a non-in-place op returning a new tensor; the previous in-place (view) behavior is available via the new `ggml_clamp_inplace` ([llama.cpp#27644](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27644))                                                               
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           
   ### Core changes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           
   - Fix `ggml_clamp` to be a proper non-in-place op, add `ggml_clamp_inplace`, and allow in-place clamp in the allocator ([llama.cpp#27644](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27644))                                                                                            
   - Optimize CPU `concat` by replacing per-element copies with row-level `memcpy` ([llama.cpp#24575](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/24575))                                                                                                                                   
   - Meta backend: add tensor-split mode, including delayed all-reduce for independent shared-expert branches ([llama.cpp#26490](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26490))                                                                                                        
   - Meta backend: fix tensor split-state propagation for `mul_mat`, reshape, flash-attn and other ops ([llama.cpp#27574](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27574))                                                                                                               
   - Shorten virtual device naming in the CUDA and Metal backends ([llama.cpp#27608](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27608))                                                                                                                                                    
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           
   ### Backend changes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           
   #### CUDA                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           
   - Add `POOL_1D` op support ([llama.cpp#27573](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27573))                                                                                                                                                                                        
   - Shorten virtual device naming ([llama.cpp#27608](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27608))                                                                                                                                                                                   
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           
   #### Metal                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           
   - Split the monolithic kernel source into per-op files and compile them in parallel ([llama.cpp#26561](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26561))                                                                                                                               
   - Per-device tuned (Q, NE) flash-attention vector sizes ([llama.cpp#26570](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26570))                                                                                                                                                           
   - Shorten virtual device naming ([llama.cpp#27608](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27608))                                                                                                                                                                                   
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           
   #### SYCL                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           
   - Re-add Q2_K reordered MMVQ and ESIMD kernels ([llama.cpp#27490](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27490))                                                                                                                                                                    
   - Mark TQ2_0 as not supported ([llama.cpp#27660](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27660))                                                                                                                                                                                     
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           
   #### Vulkan                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           
   - Add `PAD_REFLECT_1D` op ([llama.cpp#26586](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26586))                                                                                                                                                                                         
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           
   #### OpenCL                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           
   - Fuse MoE per-expert bias adds into the GLU and combine epilogues (opt-out via env vars) ([llama.cpp#26431](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/26431))                                                                                                                         
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           
   #### WebGPU                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           
   - Fix handling of infinity values in `ARGSORT` and `TOP_K` ([llama.cpp#27538](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27538))                                                                                                                                                        
   - Reorder includes to avoid a V/K macro conflict in the KV-overlap case ([llama.cpp#27545](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/27545))

- [Releases and versioning of `ggml-org` projects](https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/discussions/1579)

# Release v0.21.0

- Title: v0.21.0
- URL: https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/releases/tag/v0.21.0
- Tag commit: `8599e0ea3756c4bac4ef813af2241cb1a8bbfb0b`
- Published: 2026-08-21 13:31 CEST
- Prerelease: false

## Overview

New version has been released.

**More info:** [dist : releases and versioning of ggml-org projects](https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/discussions/1579)

## Changelog since v0.20.2

8599e0ea sync : llama.cpp
9a538ea5 Revert "sycl : add Q2_K reordered MMVQ and ESIMD kernels (llama/26336)" (llama/27486)
28d7ab84 ci : extract build job into separate release workflow (#1598)
91147d0c ggml : bump version to 0.21.0 (#1597)
19a38580 scripts : restore release.sh preparation script (#1596)
bb83ce08 ci : replace release workflow with make-release (#1595)
5da4cf17 ci : split self-hosted jobs into separate workflow (#1594)
33be41c5 sync : llama.cpp
451e766e kleidiai : add SME2 F32 GEMV kernel support (llama/26891)
59bbab9c sycl : add Q2_K reordered MMVQ and ESIMD kernels (llama/26336)
5a232ab4 test : make the FA V-is-view-of-K case a test case parameter (llama/27394)
14dfda3a sycl : Add Q5_K ESIMD kernel (llama/26376)
df8336ef opencl: keep the vocab-scale K-quant lm_head on the CPU for Adreno A7X (compiler issue workaround) (llama/26440)
99694692 sycl: Update gate logic for Alchemist GPUs regarding OneDNN features. (llama/26635)
1d30b1b5 sycl: fix multiple warnings in compiling sycl backend (llama/26713)
7378ac17 sycl : fix load model with mlock issue (llama/27250)
4979ee24 ggml: support ggml_rope_set_offset on opencl, sycl, wgpu, hexagon (llama/27345)
33c9ea5e metal : clamp K extent in tensor API mat-mat kernel for K not a multiple of 32 (llama/27450)
9e7a4c2c opencl: fix q6_K flat mul_mat for Adreno A6x/A7x GPUs with older E031 compilers (llama/26476)
505842b4 opencl: fix local size for norm (llama/27339)
b0d45de2 vulkan: FA MMQ should use fp32 for Q quantization calculations (llama/27413)
3821f6ed metal : dequant kv cache only for large batches (llama/27438)
7159fc6a CI: Use LLVM's OpenMP over MSVC_DEBUG_non_redist on Windows (llama/26678)
02a0ab2b CUDA: adding switch points per HW and quant type to tune the mvq->MMQ decode crossover (llama/26079)
e4159ddd metal : dequantize quantized KV to F16 before flash attention (llama/27390)
9a1d2348 Revert "tensor-split meta backend fixes (#26502)" (llama/27433)
0ab579a5 ggml: fix backend split scheduler race condition (llama/26040)
ee401c8b ggml-cuda: provide static workspace for cuBLAS handles (llama/26574)
6a6a4c19 vulkan : add source groups for shaders (llama/26666)
e3e8dc16 opencl: make the MoE expert scatter deterministic (llama/26464)
7c6058cf tensor-split meta backend fixes (llama/26502)
37c79ff5 hexagon: fix FA HMX queue ordering and pack the rescale D matrices (llama/27042)
585e463c opencl: port fused ssm_scan kernel (Mamba-2, d_state in {128, 256}) to GPU (llama/26439)
ff4f3e43 ggml-cpu: gate __fp16 on __ARM_FP16_FORMAT_IEEE (llama/26860)
480cc77c vulkan : dequant q8_0 KV once in coopmat1 (llama/25494)
8fc9724d vulkan: add null checks in ggml_vk_queue_command_pools_cleanup (llama/27353)
f673848d sycl: report zero devices instead of aborting when the host has none (llama/27291)
aa9d2226 ggml: add ggml_rope_set_offset (+ metal support) (llama/27120)
868403cf metal : dequantize q8_0 using packed types (llama/27370)
55e4b3b4 vulkan: tiled transpose for 0<->2 permuted CONT (llama/26585)
52b66a4e ggml-webgpu: add mulmat with overlapping src0/src1 (e.g., for minimax-01) (llama/27321)
981a41bb opencl: fix WAR race in the generic FA tile kernels when the WG spans subgroups (llama/26434)
f16a1a2c RPC: populate use_count to enable fusion inside backends (llama/27142)
b74262dd sycl: honor GGML_HINT_SRC0_IS_HADAMARD (llama/27298)
